- **Key tolerance:** Lock-out hasp must accommodate at least 6 locks; residual voltage at motor terminals must be less than 5 V AC; hydraulic accumulator pressure must read 0 bar ±…
- **Standards:** `Act 85`, `SANS 60204-1:2016`, `SANS 12100:2010`, `ISO 2307:2019`
